* All meetings, except closed sessions, are open to the public.  The Board welcomes citizens’ comments.  Th e public participation 
portion of the meeting is limited to one -half hour for discussion of agenda items and one -half hour for non -agenda items.  Each 
person will be allowed to speak for up to three minutes, except where the number of speakers exceeds the time limit.  In those 
instances, the President may reduce the time limit for each speaker in order to accommodate all of the individuals who wish t o 
address the Board of Education.  In any event, no one’s right to address the Board will be denied.

CURRICULUM 
MHSAA Membership Resolution 
secondary schools which are under the direction of this Board 
of Education/Governing Body of the Troy School District, City 
of Troy, County of Oakland, of the state of Michigan, are 
hereby: 
(A) enrolled as members of the Michigan High School Athletic 
Association, Inc., a nonprofit association, and 
(B) are further enrolled to participate in the approved 
        interschool athletic activities sponsored by said association. 
The Board of Education/Governing Body hereby delegates to 
the Superintend ent or his/her designee(s) the responsibility for 
the supervision and control of said activities and hereby 
accepts the Constitution and By-Laws of said association and 
adopts as its own the rules, regulations and interpretations (as 
minimum standards) as published in the current HANDBOOK  as 
the governing code under which the said school(s) shall 
conduct its program of interscholastic athletics and agrees to 
primary enforcement of said rules, regulations, interpretations 
and qualifications.  In addition, it is hereby agreed that schools 
which host or participate in the association’s meets and 
tournaments shall follow and enforce all tournament policies, 
procedures and schedules.

6. B.  PERSONNEL 
CONSIDERATION:  Administrative Appointment –  
     Principal, Martell Elementary School 
I am pleased to recommend the appointment of Mr. John Pagel to the position of 
Principal at Martell Elementary School .   Mr. Pagel  has an extensive 25 -year 
career in education.  He began his educational career teaching fourth and fifth 
grade in the Algonac School District.  Mr. Pagel then served as middle school 
Assistant Principal in the Avondale School District.  Most recently, Mr.  Pagel has 
served the last 16 years as principal at Deerfield Elementary in the Avondale 
School District.  During his time in Avondale, he fulfilled many additional 
responsibilities including technology director, mass media curriculum chairperson, 
district school safety committee member, and managed the implementation of 
the technology bond.  Mr. Pagel  has a Bachelor’s Degree in Elementary 
Education from The University of Michigan and a Masters of Arts in Educational 
Administration from Wayne State University.   
With your approval, Mr. Pagel will begin his duties as Principal as of August 17, 
2016.  
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Acceptance of the resolution below:  
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by         
 Supported by        
BE IT THEREFORE RESOLVED that the Board of Education issues a two-year 
probationary administrative contract without tenure in the administrative position 
to Mr. John Pagel, who will begin his duties as Principal, Martell Elementary 
School, effective August 17, 2016.  
 Yes

 7. A.  08.16.16 
7. CURRICULUM 
A. CONSIDERATION:   Schools of Choice - Section 105c 
     9th Grade for 2017 – 2018  
In accordance with Michigan state law, school districts shall pass a resolution 
each school year if they intend to participate in a Schools of Choice program.  It 
is anticipated that seats will be available for the 2017-2018 school year for grade 
9 at the International Academy East.  It would be appropriate at this time to 
consider adopting a Schools of Choice section 105c resolution for 90 non-resident 
9th grade students who attend a district in another county, if that intermediate 
school district is contiguous with the Oakland Intermediate School District 
boundaries.   
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Adopt the following resolution. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by: __________________________ 
 Supported by: __________________________ 
Be it therefore resolved that the Board of Education of the Troy School 
District, exercising the option permitted by Section 105c  of the State School Aid 
Act of 1979, as amended by Public Act 300 of 1996, will accept applications for a 
Schools of Choice program for the 2017-2018 school year for 90 non-resident 9th 
grade students who attend a school district in another county if that 
Intermediate School District is contiguous with the Oakland Intermediate School 
District boundaries for the purposes of attending the International Academy East 
program located in the Troy School District. 
Yes: __________ 
No: __________  
IA East Enrollment History 
2011-2012 133 116 101 91 22% 441 
2012-2013 132 117 103 99 26% 451 
2013-2014 143 119 113 100 25% 475 
2014-2015 149 130 117 108 19% 504 
2015-2016 159 141 120 113 14% 533 
2016-2017 158 155 135 116 18% 564 
Current lottery information for class of 2020 
 80 Troy School District Seats (167 applicants) 
- We reached #151 on the wait list this year 
 90 Out-of-County School-of-Choice Seats (172 applicants – 78 seats filled) 
- We did reach every number on the wait list by mid-June 
Recommendation: No change to lottery process for class of 2021 
Rationale: There were more than 90 qualified applicants in the school of choice lottery, 
but many decided to choose a different school because their lottery number was high and 
they could not wait until the end of the school year to make a decision.  Without a lottery, 
our school-of-choice numbers would be too high.   
Future FTE 
If we continue to bring in classes of 155 – 170, we will need to increase our FTE at the senior 
level.  For 2017-2018, we will need approximately 0.8 – 1.0 additional FTE to support the 
increase of seniors from the current 116 to 135.  At that point, we should reach an equilibrium of 
class size and FTE.
